{
  "role": "user",
  "content": "# EPIC 0.9 — Database Schema & Data Architecture Specification\n\nMục tiêu của Epic 0.9 KHÔNG phải thiết kế lại database.\n\nDatabase hiện đã tồn tại và được xem là nguồn sự thật (Source of Truth).\n\nNhiệm vụ của bạn là xây dựng tài liệu Database Architecture chính thức dựa trên schema hiện có, đồng thời đảm bảo nó hoàn toàn phù hợp với Technical Specification v1.0.\n\nYêu cầu:\n\n## 1. Database Overview\n\n* Database engine\n* Naming conventions\n* UUID strategy\n* Timestamp strategy\n* Soft delete policy\n* Transaction strategy\n\n## 2. Entity Relationship Diagram (ERD)\n\nMô tả đầy đủ quan hệ giữa tất cả entity.\n\nVí dụ:\n\n* users\n* tutor_profiles\n* service_listings\n* bookings\n* transactions\n* withdrawals\n* complaints\n* subjects\n* reviews\n* notifications\n* audit_logs\n* admin_notes\n* ops_items\n* ...\n\nThể hiện rõ:\n\n* One-to-One\n* One-to-Many\n* Many-to-Many\n* Junction tables\n\n## 3. Table Catalog\n\nVới mỗi bảng hãy mô tả:\n\n* Purpose\n* Primary Key\n* Foreign Keys\n* Business Description\n\nKHÔNG cần ghi toàn bộ CREATE TABLE SQL.\n\n## 4. Column Definitions\n\nCho từng bảng:\n\n* Column\n* Type\n* Nullable\n* Default\n* Description\n* Constraint\n\n## 5. Foreign Key Rules\n\nLiệt kê đầy đủ:\n\n* FK\n* ON DELETE\n* ON UPDATE\n* Cascade Policy\n\n## 6. Index Strategy\n\nCho từng bảng:\n\n* Primary Index\n* Unique Index\n* Composite Index\n* Search Index\n* Audit Index\n\nGiải thích lý do tồn tại của từng index.\n\n## 7. Constraints\n\nBao gồm:\n\n* CHECK constraints\n* UNIQUE constraints\n* Business constraints\n* Status validation\n* Amount validation\n\n## 8. Soft Delete Strategy\n\nXác định rõ:\n\n* bảng nào dùng deleted_at\n* bảng nào immutable\n* bảng nào hard delete\n* include_deleted policy\n\n## 9. Data Lifecycle\n\nCho từng entity mô tả:\n\n* Created\n* Updated\n* Archived\n* Deleted\n* Audit\n\n## 10. Performance Strategy\n\nBao gồm:\n\n* Estimated row count\n* Hot tables\n* Read-heavy tables\n* Write-heavy tables\n* Index considerations\n* Pagination strategy\n\n## 11. Security\n\n* Sensitive columns\n* Masking policy\n* Encryption policy\n* PII handling\n* Audit requirements\n\n## 12. Database Standards Checklist\n\nĐối chiếu với Technical Specification v1.0 để xác nhận:\n\n* Naming Convention\n* UUID Policy\n* Timestamp Policy\n* Status Convention\n* Soft Delete Convention\n* Foreign Key Convention\n* Audit Convention\n\nNếu phát hiện điểm nào database hiện tại chưa khớp Technical Specification thì KHÔNG tự sửa.\n\nThay vào đó tạo mục:\n\n\"Specification Gap\"\n\nbao gồm:\n\n* Current Database\n* Technical Spec\n* Impact\n* Recommendation\n\nĐây chỉ là tài liệu chuẩn hóa (documentation), không phải redesign.\n\nSau khi hoàn thành, khóa tài liệu với trạng thái:\n\n**EPIC 0.9 — Database Schema & Data Architecture Specification v1.0 (LOCKED)**."

      "type": "tool_result",
      "content": "1\t-- Bảng Wallets: Lưu trữ số dư của tất cả User (Admin, Tutor, Parent, Student)\n2\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wallets (\n3\t    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n4\t    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) UNIQUE,\n5\t    balance NUMERIC(15,2) DEFAULT 0 CHECK (balance >= 0),\n6\t    held_balance NUMERIC(15,2) DEFAULT 0 CHECK (held_balance >= 0),\n7\t    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n8\t);\n9\t\n10\t-- Bảng Transactions: Lưu vết mọi biến động dòng tiền\n11\tDO $$ BEGIN\n12\t    CREATE TYPE transaction_type AS ENUM ('DEPOSIT', 'WITHDRAW', 'PAYMENT', 'REFUND', 'COMMISSION');\n13\tEXCEPTION\n14\t    WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;\n15\tEND $$;\n16\t\n17\tDO $$ BEGIN\n18\t    CREATE TYPE transaction_status AS ENUM ('PENDING', 'SUCCESS', 'FAILED', 'HELD_IN_ESCROW', 'RELEASED', 'REFUNDED', 'DISPUTED');\n19\tEXCEPTION\n20\t    WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;\n21\tEND $$;\n22\t\n23\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions (\n24\t    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n25\t    wallet_id UUID REFERENCES wallets(id),\n26\t    amount NUMERIC(15,2) NOT NULL,\n27\t    type transaction_type NOT NULL,\n28\t    status transaction_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',\n29\t    gateway TEXT, -- 'MOMO', 'VNPAY', 'SYSTEM'\n30\t    gateway_transaction_id TEXT UNIQUE, -- Mã giao dịch bên cổng thanh toán (để check trùng lặp - Idempotency)\n31\t    reference_id UUID, -- Ví dụ: ID của buổi học (lesson_id)\n32\t    description TEXT,\n33\t    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),\n34\t    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n35\t);\n36\t\n37\t-- Bảng Disputes: Quản lý khiếu nại\n38\tDO $$ BEGIN\n39\t    CREATE TYPE dispute_status AS ENUM ('OPEN', 'RESOLVED_REFUND', 'RESOLVED_RELEASE');\n40\tEXCEPTION\n41\t    WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;\n42\tEND $$;\n43\t\n44\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS disputes (\n45\t    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n46\t    transaction_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES transactions(id),\n47\t    raised_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n48\t    reason TEXT NOT NULL,\n49\t    status dispute_status DEFAULT 'OPEN',\n50\t    admin_note TEXT,\n51\t    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),\n52\t    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ\n53\t);\n54\t\n55\t-- ==========================================\n56\t-- POSTGRESQL FUNCTIONS (RPC) CHO ESCROW LOGIC\n57\t-- ==========================================\n58\t\n59\t-- 1. Nạp tiền vào ví (Chỉ gọi từ IPN Webhook)\n60\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION process_deposit(p_wallet_id UUID, p_amount NUMERIC, p_gateway TEXT, p_gateway_tx_id TEXT)\n61\tRETURNS BOOLEAN\n62\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n63\tSECURITY DEFINER\n64\tAS $$\n65\tBEGIN\n66\t    -- Kiểm tra Idempotency (Tránh cộng tiền 2 lần cho 1 mã giao dịch webhook)\n67\t    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM transactions WHERE gateway_transaction_id = p_gateway_tx_id AND status = 'SUCCESS') THEN\n68\t        RETURN FALSE; \n69\t    END IF;\n70\t\n71\t    -- Cập nhật số dư\n72\t    UPDATE wallets SET balance = balance + p_amount, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_wallet_id;\n73\t\n74\t    -- Lưu log giao dịch\n75\t    INSERT INTO transactions (wallet_id, amount, type, status, gateway, gateway_transaction_id, description)\n76\t    VALUES (p_wallet_id, p_amount, 'DEPOSIT', 'SUCCESS', p_gateway, p_gateway_tx_id, 'Nạp tiền qua ' || p_gateway);\n77\t\n78\t    RETURN TRUE;\n79\tEND;\n80\t$$;\n81\t\n82\t-- 2. Đặt lịch học (Hold Money / Escrow)\n83\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION hold_money_for_lesson(p_payer_wallet_id UUID, p_amount NUMERIC, p_lesson_id UUID)\n84\tRETURNS UUID\n85\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n86\tSECURITY DEFINER\n87\tAS $$\n88\tDECLARE\n89\t    v_tx_id UUID;\n90\tBEGIN\n91\t    -- Trừ balance khả dụng, cộng vào held_balance (Sẽ văng lỗi nếu balance < p_amount nhờ CHECK constraint)\n92\t    UPDATE wallets \n93\t    SET balance = balance - p_amount, held_balance = held_balance + p_amount \n94\t    WHERE id = p_payer_wallet_id;\n95\t\n96\t    -- Tạo transaction trạng thái HELD_IN_ESCROW\n97\t    INSERT INTO transactions (wallet_id, amount, type, status, gateway, reference_id, description)\n98\t    VALUES (p_payer_wallet_id, p_amount, 'PAYMENT', 'HELD_IN_ESCROW', 'SYSTEM', p_lesson_id, 'Đặt lịch học chờ giải ngân')\n99\t    RETURNING id INTO v_tx_id;\n100\t\n101\t    RETURN v_tx_id;\n102\tEND;\n103\t$$;\n104\t\n105\t-- 3. Giải ngân (Release Money) cho Gia sư\n106\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION release_escrow(\n107\t    p_tx_id UUID, \n108\t    p_payer_wallet_id UUID, \n109\t    p_tutor_wallet_id UUID, \n110\t    p_admin_wallet_id UUID, \n111\t    p_amount NUMERIC, \n112\t    p_commission_rate NUMERIC\n113\t)\n114\tRETURNS BOOLEAN\n115\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n116\tSECURITY DEFINER\n117\tAS $$\n118\tDECLARE\n119\t    v_commission_amount NUMERIC;\n120\t    v_tutor_amount NUMERIC;\n121\tBEGIN\n122\t    v_commission_amount := p_amount * p_commission_rate;\n123\t    v_tutor_amount := p_amount - v_commission_amount;\n124\t\n125\t    -- 1. Trừ tiền khỏi held_balance của người gửi\n126\t    UPDATE wallets SET held_balance = held_balance - p_amount WHERE id = p_payer_wallet_id;\n127\t\n128\t    -- 2. Cộng tiền cho Gia sư\n129\t    UPDATE wallets SET balance = balance + v_tutor_amount WHERE id = p_tutor_wallet_id;\n130\t\n131\t    -- 3. Cộng tiền hoa hồng cho Admin\n132\t    UPDATE wallets SET balance = balance + v_commission_amount WHERE id = p_admin_wallet_id;\n133\t\n134\t    -- 4. Cập nhật Transaction gốc thành RELEASED\n135\t    UPDATE transactions SET status = 'RELEASED', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_tx_id;\n136\t\n137\t    -- 5. Ghi log Commission\n138\t    INSERT INTO transactions (wallet_id, amount, type, status, gateway, reference_id, description)\n139\t    VALUES (p_admin_wallet_id, v_commission_amount, 'COMMISSION', 'SUCCESS', 'SYSTEM', p_tx_id, 'Phí hoa hồng từ giải ngân');\n140\t\n141\t    -- 6. Ghi log Nhận Tiền\n142\t    INSERT INTO transactions (wallet_id, amount, type, status, gateway, reference_id, description)\n143\t    VALUES (p_tutor_wallet_id, v_tutor_amount, 'PAYMENT', 'SUCCESS', 'SYSTEM', p_tx_id, 'Giải ngân thanh toán lớp học');\n144\t\n145\t    RETURN TRUE;\n146\tEND;\n147\t$$;\n148\t\n149\t-- 4. Hoàn tiền (Refund) từ Escrow về lại Người gửi\n150\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION refund_escrow(p_tx_id UUID, p_payer_wallet_id UUID, p_amount NUMERIC)\n151\tRETURNS BOOLEAN\n152\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n153\tSECURITY DEFINER\n154\tAS $$\n155\tBEGIN\n156\t    -- 1. Trừ tiền khỏi held_balance, trả lại vào balance khả dụng\n157\t    UPDATE wallets \n158\t    SET held_balance = held_balance - p_amount, balance = balance + p_amount \n159\t    WHERE id = p_payer_wallet_id;\n160\t\n161\t    -- 2. Cập nhật Transaction\n162\t    UPDATE transactions SET status = 'REFUNDED', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_tx_id;\n163\t    \n164\t    -- 3. Ghi log Hoàn Tiền\n165\t    INSERT INTO transactions (wallet_id, amount, type, status, gateway, reference_id, description)\n166\t    VALUES (p_payer_wallet_id, p_amount, 'REFUND', 'SUCCESS', 'SYSTEM', p_tx_id, 'Hoàn tiền vào ví');\n167\t\n168\t    RETURN TRUE;\n169\tEND;\n170\t$$;\n171\t\n172\t-- Hàm tự tạo ví cho mọi User đang có\n173\tINSERT INTO wallets (user_id, balance, held_balance)\n174\tSELECT id, 0, 0 FROM users\n175\tON CONFLICT (user_id) DO NOTHING;\n176\t\n177\t-- Trigger tự tạo ví khi có User mới\n178\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_wallet_for_new_user()\n179\tRETURNS TRIGGER AS $$\n180\tBEGIN\n181\t  INSERT INTO wallets (user_id, balance, held_balance)\n182\t  VALUES (NEW.id, 0, 0);\n183\t  RETURN NEW;\n184\tEND;\n185\t$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;\n186\t\n187\tDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_create_wallet ON users;\n188\tCREATE TRIGGER trigger_create_wallet\n189\tAFTER INSERT ON users\n190\tFOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION create_wallet_for_new_user();\n191\t"

Existing duplicate progress rows may need cleanup.\", error.message);\n4391\t  }\n4392\t}\n4393\t\n4394\t\n4395\tapp.get(\"/api/tutor/courses\", verifyToken, async (req, res) => {\n4396\t  try {\n4397\t    if (req.user.role !== \"tutor\") return res.status(403).json({ message: \"Tutor access only.\" });\n4398\t    await ensureCourseSchema();\n4399\t    const courses = await pool.query(\n4400\t      `SELECT c.*,\n4401\t              COUNT(DISTINCT l.id)::int AS \"lessonCount\",\n4402\t              COUNT(DISTINCT e.id)::int AS \"enrollmentCount\",\n4403\t              COALESCE(COUNT(DISTINCT e.id) FILTER (WHERE e.status = 'active') * c.price, 0)::int AS revenue\n4404\t       FROM courses c\n4405\t       LEFT JOIN course_lessons l ON l.course_id = c.id\n4406\t       LEFT JOIN course_enrollments e ON e.course_id = c.id\n4407\t       WHERE c.tutor_id = $1\n4408\t       GROUP BY c.id\n4409\t       ORDER BY c.updated_at DESC, c.created_at DESC`,\n4410\t      [req.user.userId]\n4411\t    );\n4412\t    const ids = courses.rows.map((course) => course.id);\n4413\t    const lessons = ids.length\n4414\t      ? await pool.query(\n4415\t          `SELECT *\n4416\t           FROM course_lessons\n4417\t           WHERE course_id = ANY($1::uuid[])\n4418\t           ORDER BY course_id, position ASC, created_at ASC`,\n4419\t          [ids]\n4420\t        )\n4421\t      : { rows: [] };\n4422\t    const lessonMap = lessons.rows.reduce((map, lesson) => {\n4423\t      if (!map[lesson.course_id]) map[lesson.course_id] = [];\n4424\t      map[lesson.course_id].push({"
    }
  ]
}

Engine\n\n```\nEngine:     PostgreSQL 15+ (managed by Supabase)\nProvider:   Supabase (https://supabase.com)\nExtensions: uuid-ossp (uuid_generate_v4), pgcrypto (gen_random_uuid)\nLocale:     UTF-8\nTimezone:   UTC (tất cả TIMESTAMPTZ lưu và trả về UTC)\n```\n\n### 1.2 Naming Convention (Actual State)\n\n```\nTable names:    snake_case, đa số plural\n                Exceptions: một số singular (class_members, student_tutor_interactions...)\nColumn names:   snake_case, singular\nPrimary keys:   Luôn là `id UUID`\nForeign keys:   {entity}_id pattern (phần lớn) — một số dùng semantic name\nIndexes:        idx_{table}_{column} pattern (được tuân thủ)\n```\n\n### 1.3 UUID Strategy\n\n```\nGenerator:  gen_random_uuid() (pgcrypto, UUID v4)\n            schema_lesson_feedbacks.sql dùng uuid_generate_v4() — khác generator\n            nhưng cùng UUID v4 format\nDefault:    TẤT CẢ tables đều có UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()\nFormat:     xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx (128-bit random)\n```\n\n### 1.4 Timestamp Strategy\n\n```\nType:       TIMESTAMPTZ (timestamp with time zone) — đúng chuẩn Technical Spec\n            Ngoại lệ: function — được tạo một lần, reuse\n            cho tất cả tables có updated_at\nPattern:    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n```\n\n### 1.5 Soft Delete Policy (Actual State)\n\n**Hiện tại:** Không có bảng nào implement `deleted_at` column.\n\nThay thế đang dùng:\n- `is_banned BOOLEAN` — users table (user_management_migration.sql)\n- `status` column với archived/cancelled/rejected states — hầu hết entities\n- `is_published BOOLEAN` — exam_papers\n\n### 1.6 Migration File Registry\n\nCơ sở dữ liệu được tạo từ các files theo thứ columns\n─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nclass_members            classes         users(student)  joined_at\nparent_children          users(parent)   users(student)  nickname, linked_at\ncourse_enrollments       courses         users(student)  status, purchased_at\nstudent_tutor_interactions users(student) users(tutor)   is_favorite, notes\nlearning_paths           classes         users(student)  current_level, target_level, ...\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 3 — TABLE CATALOG\n\n### Group 1 — Identity & Auth\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs | Notes |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|-------|\n| `users` | Tài khoản toàn hệ thống — student, parent, tutor, admin | `id UUID` | — | Role-based single table; `is_banned` column từ migration |\n| `tutor_profiles` | Hồ sơ gia sư — thông tin dạy học + trạng thái duyệt | `id UUID` | `user_id → users` | 1:1 với users; nhiều columns được ADD sau qua migrations |\n| `tutor_certificates` | Chứng chỉ của gia sư — nhiều cert mỗi tutor | `id UUID` | `tutor_profile_id → tutor_profiles` | Auto-migrated từ server.js |\n| `login_logs` | Log đăng nhập — IP, user agent, flag suspicious | `id UUID` | `user_id → users` | Auto-migrated từ server.js |\n| `wallets` | Ví điện tử — balance + held_balance (escrow) | `id UUID` | `user_id → users` UNIQUE | 1:1 với users; trigger tạo tự động khi user mới |\n| `student_link_codes` | Mã 8 ký tự cho phụ huynh liên kết với học sinh | `id UUID` | `student_id → users` UNIQUE | 1:1 với student |\n| `parent_children` | Liên kết phụ huynh ↔ học sinh | `id UUID` | `parent_id, student_id → users` | N:N junction table |\n\n### Group 2 — Class Workspace (classes-based)\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `classes` | Lớp học online do tutor tạo | `id UUID` | `tutor_id → users` |\n| `class_members` | Học sinh trong lớp | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `student_id → users` |\n| `materials` | Tài liệu học tập (class-based) | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `uploaded_by → users` |\n| `assignments` | Bài tập (class-based) | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `created_by → users` |\n| `lessons` | Bài học (class-based) | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `material_id → materials` |\n| `discussions` | Bài đăng thảo luận trong lớp | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `user_id → users` |\n| `discussion_replies` | Phản hồi bài thảo luận | `id UUID` | `discussion_id → discussions`, `user_id → users` |\n| `learning_paths` | Lộ trình học cá nhân (AI-generated) | `id UUID` | `class_id → classes`, `student_id → users` |\n| `learning_path_steps` | Từng bước trong lộ trình | `id UUID` | `learning_path_id → learning_paths`, `lesson_id → lessons` |\n\n### Group 3 — Course Module (canonical v2)\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `courses` | Khóa học do tutor tạo + publish | `id UUID` | `tutor_id → users` |\n| `course_lessons` | Bài học trong khóa học | `id UUID` | `course_id → courses` |\n| `course_enrollments` | Học sinh đăng ký khóa học | `id UUID` | `course_id → courses`, `student_id → users` |\n| `course_progress` | Tiến độ từng bài học | `id UUID` | `enrollment_id → course_enrollments`, `lesson_id → course_lessons` |\n\n### Group 4 — Finance\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `transactions` | Mọi biến động tiền — ENUM type | `id UUID` | `wallet_id → wallets` |\n| `disputes` | Khiếu nại tài chính | `id UUID` | `transaction_id → transactions`, `raised_by → users` |\n| `invoices` | Hóa đơn học phí — phụ huynh | `id UUID` | `parent_id, student_id, tutor_id → users` |\n| `coupons` | Mã giảm giá | `id UUID` | — |\n| `wishlists` | Bookmark khóa học / gia sư | `id UUID` | `user_id → users` |\n\n### Group 5 — Matching & Interaction\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `tutor_requests` | Yêu cầu tìm gia sư (14-step wizard) | `id UUID` | `student_id → users` (nullable — guest allowed) |\n| `student_tutor_interactions` | Yêu thích + ghi chú cá nhân | `id UUID` | `student_id, tutor_id → users` |\n| `tutor_sessions` | Lịch học / buổi dạy | `id UUID` | `student_id, tutor_id → users` |\n| `tutor_reviews` | Nhận xét định kỳ của gia sư | `id UUID` | `student_id, tutor_id → users` |\n| `lesson_feedbacks` | Feedback từng buổi học | `id UUID` | `tutor_id, student_id` (không có FK constraint) |\n| `reviews` | Đánh giá platform (generic) | `id UUID` | `reviewer_id → users` (SET NULL) |\n\n### Group 6 — Assessment\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `quizzes` | Đề thi chính thức | `id UUID` | `created_by → users` |\n| `quiz_questions` | Câu hỏi trong đề | `id UUID` | `quiz_id → quizzes` |\n| `quiz_attempts` | Bài làm của học sinh | `id UUID` | `quiz_id → quizzes`, `student_id → users` |\n| `practice_sessions` | Phiên ôn luyện AI | `id UUID` | `student_id → users` |\n| `exam_papers` | Đề thi upload bởi gia sư | `id UUID` | `uploaded_by → users` (SET NULL) |\n| `exam_paper_questions` | Câu hỏi đề thi upload | `id UUID` | `exam_paper_id → exam_papers` |\n| `exam_paper_attempts` | Lần làm bài đề thi upload | `id UUID` | `exam_paper_id, student_id` |\n\n### Group 7 — Communication\n\n| Table | Purpose | PK | Key FKs |\n|-------|---------|-----|---------|\n| `messages` | Nhắn tin 1:1 (parent ↔ tutor) | `id UUID` | `sender_id, receiver_id → users` |\n| `notifications` | Thông báo cho user | `id UUID` | `user_id → users` |\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 4 — COLUMN DEFINITIONS\n\n### 4.1 users\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | Description | Constraint index:\n\n```\ntransactions.wallet_id          → Cần để query transactions by wallet\ntransactions.status             → Cần để admin filter by status\ndisputes.transaction_id         → Join thường xuyên\ndisputes.status                 → Filter open disputes\nwallets.user_id                 → Có UNIQUE constraint (implicit index OK)\nquiz_attempts.student_id + quiz_id → Composite: check if student already attempted\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 7 — CONSTRAINTS\n\n### 7.1 CHECK Constraints\n\n```\nTable                 Column         IN ('scheduled','completed','cancelled','absent','late')\ntutor_reviews         rating              BETWEEN 1 AND 5\ninvoices              status              IN ('pending','paid','overdue','cancelled')\n                      invoice_no          UNIQUE\nwallets               balance             >= 0\nwallets               held_balance        >= Constraints\n\n```\nusers.email                         Không thể 2 accounts cùng email\nusers.google_id                     Không thể 2 accounts cùng Google account\nwallets.user_id                     1 ví per user\ninvoices.invoice_no                 Số hóa đơn duy nhất\ntransactions.gateway_transaction_id Idempotency — 1 gateway txn = 1 record\nstudent_link_codes.student_id       1 học sinh = 1 link code\nstudent_link_codes.code             Code globally unique\nclass_members(class_id, student_id) Không join 2 lần\ncourse_enrollments(course_id, student_id) Không mua 2 lần\ncourse_progress(enrollment_id, lesson_id) 1 progress record per lesson\nlearning_paths(class_id, student_id) 1 path per student per class\nstudent_tutor_interactions(student_id, tutor_id) 1 interaction record per pair\nparent_children(parent_id, student_id) Không link 2 lần\nwishlists(user_id, item_type, item_id) Không wishlist cùng item 2 lần\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 8 — SOFT DELETE STRATEGY\n\n### 8.1 Current Implementation\n\n```\nStrategy hiện tại của EduX:\n  KHÔNG có deleted_at column ở bất kỳ table nào.\n  Thay thế bằng status-based archival hoặc is_banned flag.\n```\n\n### 8.2 Table Classification\n\n```\nHARD DELETE (xóa thực sự — khi CASCADE ON DELETE):\n  class_members (khi class hoặc student bị xóa)\n  course_enrollments (khi course hoặc student bị xóa)\n  course_progress (theo cascade)\n  discussion_replies (khi discussion bị xóa)\n  learning_path_steps (khi learning_path bị xóa)\n  quiz_questions (khi quiz bị xóa)\n\nSTATUS-BASED ARCHIVAL (không có deleted_at, dùng status):\n  users           → is_banned = TRUE (không xóa)\n  courses         → status = 'archived'\n  classes         → status = 'archived'\n  tutor_profiles  → status = 'rejected' (tương đương disabled)\n  invoices        → status = 'cancelled'\n  tutor_sessions  → status = 'cancelled'\n\nIMMUTABLE (không xóa — financial/audit records):\n  transactions    → chỉ UPDATE status, không xóa\n  disputes        → không xóa\n  quiz_attempts   → không xóa\n  exam_paper_attempts → không xóa\n  login_logs      → không xóa\n\nNO POLICY (có thể xóa freely):\n  reviews         → hard delete nếu admin remove\n  messages        → không rõ policy\n  notifications   → nên có expiry/cleanup\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 9 — DATA LIFECYCLE\n\n```\nEntity              Created By          Updated By                Archived/Deleted\n────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nusers               User signup         User profile edit         is_banned=TRUE\ntutor_profiles      Tutor signup form   Admin approval / Tutor    status=rejected\ntutor_certificates  Tutor upload        N/A (new upload)          Cascade with profile\nclasses             Tutor              Tutor edit                 status=archived\ncourses             Tutor              Tutor edit                 status=archived/rejected\ncourse_enrollments  Student purchase    Admin/system refund       status=refunded/cancelled\ntransactions        System (functions)  System (status change)    IMMUTABLE\ndisputes            User raise          Admin resolve             resolved_at set\nwallets             Auto-trigger on user create  Functions       N/A (permanent)\ntutor_requests      Student wizard      System (match_status)     match_status=closed\ninvoices            Tutor create        Parent pays               status=cancelled\nnotifications       System events       User reads                is_read=TRUE (no cleanup)\nmessages            User send           N/A                       Hard delete (no policy)\nquiz_attempts       Student start quiz  Student submit            IMMUTABLE\nlesson_feedbacks    Tutor after lesson  N/A                       RLS protected\nreviews             User submit         N/A                       Admin hard delete\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 10 — PERFORMANCE STRATEGY\n\n### 10.1 Estimated Row Counts (Year 1 projections)\n\n```\nTable                    Estimated rows    Growth rate\n─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nusers                    50,000            +5K/month\ntutor_profiles           3,000             +200/month\ncourses (v2)             5,000             +500/month\ncourse_lessons           50,000            +5K/month\ncourse_enrollments       200,000           +20K/month\ncourse_progress          2,000,000         +200K/month (hot write table)\ntransactions             500,000           +50K/month (hot)\nwallets                  50,000            +5K/month\nmessages                 1,000,000         +100K/month (hot)\nnotifications            2,000,000         +200K/month (hot write, fast decay)\ntutor_requests           10,000            +1K/month\nquiz_attempts            100,000           +10K/month\nlogin_logs               500,000           +50K/month (hot write)\n```\n\n### 10.2 Table Classification\n\n```\nHOT WRITE TABLES (cần optimize write path):\n  course_progress     → updated mỗi khi student xem video\n  transactions        → mỗi payment event\n  notifications       → mỗi system event\n  login_logs          → mỗi login\n  messages            → realtime chat\n\nREAD-HEAVY TABLES:\n  tutor_profiles      → hiển thị trên search results, matching\n  courses             → course catalog\n  quiz_questions      → loaded khi start quiz\n  exam_paper_questions → loaded khi start exam\n\nADMIN-QUERY TABLES (complex JOIN, occasional):\n  transactions        → financial reports\n  course_enrollments  → revenue per course\n  quiz_attempts       → student performance analytics\n```\n\n### 10.3 Pagination Strategy\n\n```\nTất cả list queries phải dùng OFFSET/LIMIT với index-backed ORDER BY.\n\nRecommended ORDER BY columns:\n  users:                created_at DESC (indexed via idx chưa có → CẦN ADD)\n  tutor_profiles:       created_at DESC + status filter\n  courses:              updated_at DESC\n  transactions:         created_at DESC\n  notifications:        created_at DESC (user_id + created_at → composite index cần thêm)\n\nKeyset pagination (cho hot tables):\n  notifications:  WHERE id < :last_id AND user_id = :uid ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20\n  messages:       WHERE created_at < :last_ts ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 11 — SECURITY\n\n### 11.1 Sensitive Columns\n\n```\nTable               Column              Sensitivity     Policy\n────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nusers               password_hash       CRITICAL        Never return in API response\nusers               email               HIGH            Admin-only; hash before logging\ntutor_profiles      cccd_url            HIGH            Signed URL, 15 min expiry; audit access\ntutor_profiles      phone               MEDIUM          Not exposed in public tutor profile\ntutor_certificates  url                 MEDIUM          Signed URL for admin view\ntransactions        gateway_transaction_id MEDIUM       Internal only; not exposed to student\nwallets             balance             MEDIUM          Only visible to wallet owner + admin\ninvoices            amount              MEDIUM          Parent + admin only\nmessages            content             HIGH            Sender + receiver + admin only\npractice_sessions   tutor_feedback      MEDIUM          Tutor + student + parent only\nlesson_feedbacks    tutor_note          MEDIUM          Tutor + student + parent; RLS enforced\n```\n\n### 11.2 Masking Policy\n\n```\nIn API responses:\n  password_hash:          NEVER returned (SELECT không bao gồm column này)\n  cccd_url:               Trả về Signed URL (15 phút), không phải raw storage URL\n  certificate_url:        Trả về Signed URL\n  phone:                  Chỉ hiển thị với admin và owner\n\nIn logs / audit:\n  password_hash:          NEVER logged\n  cccd_url:               Log action \"document_viewed\", không log URL\n  bank account:           Log last 4 digits only (khi có withdrawals table)\n  email:                  Log hashed trong security events\n```\n\n### 11.3 Row Level Security\n\n```\nHiện có RLS:\n  lesson_feedbacks  → RLS enabled (Supabase)\n                      Policy 1: student/parent chỉ SELECT của mình\n                      Policy 2: tutor có thể INSERT, SELECT của mình\n\nChưa có RLS (tất cả bảng còn lại):\n  Access control được enforce bởi JWT middleware trong server.js\n  Không có RLS ở database level → nếu connection bị bypass, data exposed\n\nRecommendation (không thực hiện trong spec này — xem Specification Gaps):\n  Cần enable RLS trên: messages, notifications, wallets, transactions\n```\n\n### 11.4 PII Handling\n\n```\nPII columns:  full_name, email, phone, birthday, cccd_url, picture\n              first_name, last_name, city, country (tutor_profiles)\n\nGDPR-relevant tables:\n  users, tutor_profiles, messages, login_logs, lesson_feedbacks\n\nDeletion policy (hiện chưa có chính thức):\n  users: soft-disable (is_banned) — không hard delete\n  Recommendation: cần Right to Erasure policy → anonymize PII trên request\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 12 — DATABASE STANDARDS CHECKLIST\n\nĐối chiếu với Technical Specification v1.0 (Section 4):\n\n| Tiêu chí | Tech Spec | Database Actual | Status |\n|---------|-----------|-----------------|--------|\n| Table names | snake_case, plural | snake_case, phần lớn plural | ✓ Mostly compliant |\n| Column names | snake_case, singular | snake_case, singular | ✓ Compliant |\n| UUID PK | `id UUID DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` | `id UUID DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` | ✓ Compliant |\n| UUID generator | gen_random_uuid() | gen_random_uuid() (hầu hết) | ⚠️ lesson_feedbacks dùng uuid_generate_v4() |\n| Timestamps | `created_at`, `updated_at`, TIMESTAMPTZ | TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() | ✓ Compliant |\n| Auto-update trigger | Nên có trigger | Có: `update_updated_at()` function + triggers | ✓ Compliant |\n| Soft delete | `deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL` | Không có deleted_at — dùng status/is_banned | ✗ Gap |\n| Status values | SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE | lowercase (pending, active, approved...) | ✗ Gap |\n| Amount (VND) | NUMERIC(15, 0) integer | NUMERIC(15,2) với 2 decimal | ⚠️ Gap (minor) |\n| FK naming | `{entity}_id` | `{entity}_id` | ✓ Compliant |\n| Semantic FK names | Allowed: `assigned_to`, `created_by` | Có: `created_by`, `uploaded_by`, `raised_by` | ✓ Compliant |\n| Index naming | `idx_{table}_{columns}` | `idx_{table}_{column}` | ✓ Compliant |\n| Unique index naming | `uniq_{table}_{column}` | Mix: UNIQUE constraint + UNIQUE INDEX | ⚠️ Inconsistent |\n| Immutable tables | transactions, audit_logs | transactions (ENUM-typed, không soft delete) | ✓ Partial |\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 13 — SPECIFICATION GAPS\n\nGap là sự khác biệt giữa Technical Specification v1.0 và trạng thái database thực tế. **Không tự động sửa** — chỉ ghi nhận.\n\n---\n\n### GAP-001 — Status Values: Lowercase vs SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  tutor_profiles.status:  'pending', 'approved', 'rejected'\n  classes.status:         'active', 'archived', 'draft'\n  courses.status:         'draft', 'pending_review', 'published', 'rejected', 'archived'\n  invoices.status:        'pending', 'paid', 'overdue', 'cancelled'\n  Tất cả status values dùng lowercase\n\nTechnical Spec (Section 4.6):\n  Status values phải là SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE: PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED...\n\nImpact:\n  HIGH — Code phải dùng lowercase strings khi compare.\n  Nếu sau này API trả về SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE (theo spec) nhưng DB lưu lowercase\n  → phải convert ở application layer → dễ sót.\n\nRecommendation:\n  Option A (ít xâm phạm): Giữ nguyên DB lowercase, document rõ \"DB uses lowercase status values\".\n                           API response converter maps to SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE.\n  Option B (đúng spec):   Migration UPDATE tất cả status values + ALTER CHECK constraints.\n                           Requires migration file + update all queries.\n  Chọn Option A cho MVP để không break existing code.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-002 — Soft Delete: deleted_at không tồn tại\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  Không có deleted_at column ở bất kỳ table nào.\n  users dùng is_banned BOOLEAN thay thế.\n  Entities dùng status = 'archived'/'cancelled'/'rejected' thay thế.\n\nTechnical Spec (Section 4.8):\n  users, tutor_profiles, service_listings, bookings, transactions, complaints\n  phải có deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL\n\nImpact:\n  MEDIUM — Admin không thể view \"deleted\" records (vì không có deleted_at).\n  GDPR \"right to erasure\" chưa có cơ chế rõ ràng.\n  include_deleted=true API param chưa hoạt động được.\n\nRecommendation:\n  Thêm deleted_at khi build từng Epic. Ưu tiên: users → tutor_profiles → courses.\n  Không retroactively add vào tất cả tables một lúc.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-003 — courses Table Conflict (instructor_name vs tutor_id)\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  course_detail_schema.sql tạo: courses với instructor_name TEXT NOT NULL\n  server.js auto-migrate tạo:   courses với tutor_id UUID REFERENCES users(id)\n  \n  Nếu course_detail_schema.sql chạy TRƯỚC server.js:\n    → courses table tồn tại với instructor_name\n    → server.js ALTER TABLE thêm tutor_id (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS)\n    → cả hai columns tồn tại cùng lúc\n    → instructor_name NOT NULL nhưng không có FK → data integrity risk\n\nTechnical Spec:\n  courses.instructor_name TEXT phải được xóa → thay bằng tutor_id UUID FK\n\nImpact:\n  HIGH — Matching queries join courses → tutors sẽ fail nếu dùng instructor_name.\n  Không biết row nào đang dùng instructor_name vs tutor_id.\n\nRecommendation (P0 — đã ghi trong Implementation Plan Epic 0):\n  1. Check existing data: SELECT id, instructor_name, tutor_id FROM courses WHERE tutor_id IS NULL\n  2. Migration: UPDATE courses SET tutor_id = (SELECT id FROM users WHERE full_name = instructor_name) WHERE tutor_id IS NULL\n  3. ALTER TABLE courses ALTER COLUMN tutor_id SET NOT NULL (sau khi tất cả rows có FK)\n  4. ALTER TABLE courses DROP COLUMN instructor_name\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-004 — lessons/assignments/materials Table Conflict\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  schema.sql định nghĩa:\n    lessons(id, class_id, ...) — FK tới classes\n    assignments(id, class_id, ...) — FK tới classes\n    materials(id, class_id, ...) — FK tới classes\n\n  course_detail_schema.sql định nghĩa:\n    lessons(id, course_id, ...) — FK tới courses\n    assignments(id, course_id, ...) — FK tới courses\n    materials(id, course_id, ...) — FK tới courses\n\n  Vì cả hai dùng CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS:\n  → File chạy sau bị bỏ qua (bảng đã tồn tại)\n  → Actual schema phụ thuộc vào ORDER chạy migrations\n  → Nếu schema.sql chạy trước: lessons dùng class_id (không có course_id column)\n  → Nếu course_detail_schema.sql chạy trước: lessons dùng course_id (không có class_id column)\n\nTechnical Spec:\n  Canonical: course_id cho course-based content; class_id cho class-based content.\n  Hai use cases khác nhau nên tách thành hai tables riêng biệt.\n\nImpact:\n  CRITICAL — course_detail_schema.sql queries sẽ fail nếu lessons table là class-based.\n  Không thể load course lessons cho student nếu sai schema.\n\nRecommendation (P0 — đã ghi trong Implementation Plan Epic 0):\n  Tách thành tên riêng biệt:\n  - Giữ: lessons (class_id) — cho Class Workspace\n  - Đổi tên: course_lessons (course_id) — đã có trong server.js auto-migrate\n  - Giữ: materials (class_id) — cho Class Workspace\n  - Đổi tên: course_materials (course_id) nếu cần (hoặc materials column trong course_lessons)\n  - Giữ: assignments (class_id) — cho Class Workspace\n  - Đổi tên: course_assignments (course_id) nếu cần\n\n  Lưu ý: server.js đã có course_lessons (riêng) — đây là canonical table cho course content.\n  course_detail_schema.sql phải được deprecated và không chạy nữa.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-005 — transactions Dùng ENUM Type Thay Vì TEXT + CHECK\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  payment_migration.sql tạo PostgreSQL ENUM types:\n    transaction_type:   DEPOSIT | WITHDRAW | PAYMENT | REFUND | COMMISSION\n    transaction_status: PENDING | SUCCESS | FAILED | HELD_IN_ESCROW | RELEASED | REFUNDED | DISPUTED\n\nTechnical Spec (Section 4.4):\n  \"Không dùng ENUM type — easier to alter. Dùng TEXT với CHECK constraint.\"\n\nImpact:\n  LOW-MEDIUM — PostgreSQL ENUM cần ALTER TYPE để thêm value mới (không thể dùng\n  simple ALTER TABLE). Nếu cần thêm transaction type mới (ví dụ: CASHBACK),\n  phải ALTER TYPE — phức tạp hơn ALTER TABLE với TEXT + CHECK.\n\nRecommendation:\n  Giữ nguyên ENUM ở MVP (không rollback existing production data).\n  Khi thêm value mới: ALTER TYPE transaction_type ADD VALUE 'NEW_VALUE'.\n  Document rõ: type column là ENUM, không phải TEXT.\n  Ưu tiên migrate sang TEXT + CHECK trong future major version.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-006 — wallets Thiếu created_at Column\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  wallets table không có created_at column (chỉ có updated_at).\n\nTechnical Spec (Section 4.2):\n  Bắt buộc: id, created_at, updated_at trên TẤT CẢ tables.\n\nImpact:\n  LOW — Không thể biết ví được tạo lúc nào.\n  Không ảnh hưởng business logic.\n\nRecommendation:\n  ALTER TABLE wallets ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW();\n  Existing rows sẽ có created_at = time of migration (không phải time of creation).\n  Document caveat này.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-007 — lesson_feedbacks Thiếu FK Constraints\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  lesson_feedbacks:\n    lesson_id UUID NOT NULL  → không có REFERENCES\n    tutor_id  UUID NOT NULL  → không có REFERENCES\n    student_id UUID NOT NULL → không có REFERENCES\n\nTechnical Spec:\n  FK constraints bắt buộc cho referential integrity.\n\nImpact:\n  MEDIUM — Có thể tạo feedback với tutor_id/student_id không tồn tại.\n  Orphaned records nếu user bị xóa.\n\nRecommendation:\n  ALTER TABLE lesson_feedbacks\n    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_lf_tutor    FOREIGN KEY (tutor_id)   REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_lf_student  FOREIGN KEY (student_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;\n  lesson_id reference cần quyết định: references lessons(id) hay tutor_sessions(id) — ambiguous.\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-008 — Amount Type: NUMERIC(15,2) vs NUMERIC(15,0)\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  wallets.balance: NUMERIC(15,2) — cho phép thập phân (VD: 50000.50)\n  transactions.amount: NUMERIC(15,2) — tương tự\n  invoices.amount: BIGINT — integer VND\n\nTechnical Spec (Section 3.1):\n  Amount (VND): NUMERIC(15, 0) — integer, không thập phân\n\nImpact:\n  LOW — VND không có thập phân trong thực tế giao dịch.\n  Nếu gateway trả về fractional amounts → stored correctly nhưng unexpected.\n  Application layer phải ROUND hoặc FLOOR.\n\nRecommendation:\n  Giữ nguyên NUMERIC(15,2) — không break anything.\n  Application layer enforce integer amounts trước khi write.\n  Document: \"DB stores 2 decimal places but business logic enforces integer VND.\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### GAP-009 — reviews Table Thiếu tutor_id FK\n\n```\nCurrent Database:\n  reviews table được dùng cho platform-level testimonials (seed data: 10 reviews).\n  Không có tutor_id column → không biết review cho tutor nào.\n  Không có booking_id FK → không verify purchased/booked trước khi review.\n\nTechnical Spec (Architecture v2):\n  Reviews phải linked tới tutor + booking (verified reviews only).\n\nImpact:\n  MEDIUM — Admin không thể surface \"reviews per tutor\" từ bảng này.\n  Rating system cho tutors hiện không có backing table.\n  POST /api/tutors/matches hardcodes rating=0 vì không có reviews data.\n\nRecommendation (P0 trong Epic 0):\n  ALTER TABLE reviews\n    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tutor_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS booking_id UUID,  -- references bookings khi có table\n    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_published BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE;\n  Sau khi thêm tutor_id → fix matching query để JOIN reviews theo tutor_id.\n```\n\n---\n\n**EPIC 0.9 — Database Schema & Data Architecture Specification v1.0**\n**Status: LOCKED**\n\nTài liệu này phản ánh trạng thái database thực tế tính đến ngày review. Tất cả 9 Specification Gaps đã được ghi nhận — không có thay đổi nào được thực hiện trong tài liệu này. Các Gap sẽ được xử lý theo thứ tự ưu tiên trong Epic 0 (P0) và các Epic tiếp theo."

There's no rollback capability across any layer and no migration tracking table to know which migrations have already been applied.\n\nNow I'm mapping out the entity relationships across the seven functional domains, starting with the identity domain where users act as the central hub connecting tutor profiles, certificates, wallets, and login logs. course_enrollments + transactions)\n  refunds (1:1 → transactions)\n  wallets (1:1 → users)\n\nCONTENT & RESOURCES DOMAIN\n  resources (standalone or class_id FK)\n  resource_categories (1:N → resources)\n  certificates (1:1 → users + course_enrollments)\n\nANALYTICS & LOGGING DOMAIN\n  activity_logs (N:1 → users)\n  analytics_events (standalone event tracking)\n\nI'm mapping out the remaining domains—course progress tracking, assessments with quizzes and exams, the tutor matching and interaction system, financial transactions with wallets and disputes, content resources, and analytics logging to complete the schema picture. Generating markdown table... Generating markdown table... Generating markdown table... Generating markdown table... Generating markdown table... Generating markdown table... Defining foreign key constraints... Generating markdown table... Mapping out the remaining domains—the Matching & Interaction domain handles tutor requests, sessions, and reviews, while the Finance domain manages wallets, transactions, and payment-related entities. Both depend on the users table, and Finance also connects to courses through wishlists. Now I'm starting to visualize how these domains relate across the system, with users as a central hub connecting to identity, tutoring, and financial operations. Mapping out the remaining relationships for courses and wallets, then identifying which tables serve multiple domains like users, wallets, and courses that are shared across the system.\n\nNow I'm documenting the orphan risk tables where foreign key constraints are missing—like lesson_feedbacks and reviews that rely on application-layer validation instead—and starting to catalog the index strategy across the schema.\n\nI'm seeing several implicit unique indexes on user_id fields and email, but there are notable gaps where high-frequency queries lack proper indexing: transactions.wallet_id, tutor_requests.student_id and match_status, message sender/receiver IDs, and the class_members composite lookups all need attention.\n\nNow I'm looking at the constraints and enum types defined across the schema. There are CHECK constraints enforcing valid values for user roles, tutor statuses, lesson feedback ratings, and Vietnamese language options for homework completion. PostgreSQL ENUM types are also defined in the payment migration file to handle these categorical values.\n\nI'm also noting the soft delete strategy — EduX doesn't use a `deleted_at` column, instead relying on status flags like `is_banned` for users and `status = 'rejected'` for tutor profiles to effectively archive records, while financial data is retained permanently.\n\nThere are some data retention gaps I'm tracking: `login_logs` lacks any TTL or automated cleanup, assessment seed data is embedded directly in migrations, and there's no documented cascade delete strategy if a user account is removed. Looking at the high-volume tables, `transactions`, `login_logs`, and `messages` are growing rapidly without partitioning or archival strategies in place, and the escrow stored functions need performance review.\n\nOn the security side, only `lesson_feedbacks` has row-level security enabled—everything else depends on application-layer JWT validation. Password hashes are properly bcrypted and excluded from API responses, but wallet balances and transaction amounts rely solely on middleware checks, and message content has no encryption at rest documented. I'm also noting specification gaps where the current schema diverges from the Technical Specification v1.0.\n\nThe first major gap is status value casing—all tables use lowercase values like `pending` and `approved`, but the spec mandates SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE like `PENDING` and `APPROVED`. This is classified as P1 since it doesn't block development but creates technical debt, and it affects how API responses are formatted.\n\nThe resolution needs to happen at the architecture level before Sprint 3 starts. Either the spec gets updated to accept lowercase as the canonical format, or we introduce a status mapping layer at the API boundary to normalize values. Whichever approach we choose has to be applied consistently across all tables—mixing conventions isn't an option.\n\nThe second gap is the absence of soft delete support. Currently there's no `deleted_at` column on any table, and archival is being handled through status fields instead.\n\nHard deletes mean no audit trail and potential orphaned records if cascade deletes aren't handled carefully. The spec recommends adding `deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ`, but before implementing that, we need to decide whether soft delete is actually required for the business model. For a Tutor Marketplace, financial records absolutely need soft delete for compliance and recovery, but it might not be necessary everywhere. The better approach is to define the soft delete strategy domain by domain—Identity and Finance domains definitely need it, but other areas might not—then update the spec accordingly before Epic 1 kicks off.\n\nNow there's a schema mismatch in the courses table: one definition has `instructor_name TEXT` with no foreign key, while another has `tutor_id UUID` pointing to users but no instructor_name column. Whichever ran first won, so we might have both columns, just one, or neither depending on migration order. This blocks the Tutor Center Epic since we can't build features without a clear relationship between courses and tutors.\n\nThe core issue is that all course-tutor queries break without the `tutor_id` foreign key, and storing instructor names as free text creates data inconsistency problems. The solution is to establish `tutor_id UUID FK → users(id)` as the canonical relationship, with `instructor_name` as an optional denormalized column for display only. The Tech Lead needs to decide whether to backfill data, drop the conflicting definition, or run a one-time correction—but this must be resolved as a P0 priority before the Course and Tutor Epic can proceed.\n\nThere's another critical schema conflict affecting lessons, assignments, and materials: two different schema files define these tables with conflicting foreign keys—one pointing to `classes(id)` and the other to `courses(id)`. Because of `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, whichever file runs first wins, leaving the actual structure ambiguous in production. This means Course Module queries expecting `course_id` on these tables will silently fail if the class-based version loaded first.\n\nThe resolution is architectural: keep the class-based tables as-is for the Class Workspace Domain, but recognize that `course_detail_schema.sql` is now superseded—the course content purpose is already served by the `course_lessons` table defined in server.js. The Tech Lead needs to confirm this interpretation and formally deprecate the conflicting schema file.\n\nNow looking at the transactions table issue: the current ENUM types for `transaction_type` and `transaction_status` create operational friction when evolving the value set, since `ALTER TYPE` operations can't be easily rolled back in production. Before starting the Finance Domain Epic, I need to decide whether to migrate these to TEXT columns with CHECK constraints for better flexibility, or accept the ENUM overhead as a trade-off for type safety.\n\nFor the wallets table, there's a straightforward gap: `created_at` is missing entirely, which violates the spec requirement that all tables include this timestamp column for audit purposes.\n\nSince wallet creation is tied to user account creation through a trigger, adding `created_at` is a non-breaking change that can be handled opportunistically during any Finance Domain work without blocking feature development.\n\nMoving to the next issue, `lesson_feedbacks` has a more serious problem—the foreign key columns `lesson_id`, `tutor_id`, and `student_id` are defined as NOT NULL UUIDs but lack actual FK constraints to their respective tables, creating referential integrity gaps that the RLS policies depend on. session entity.\n\nThere's another precision mismatch in the monetary columns — the database uses `NUMERIC(15,2)` to support decimals, but the spec calls for `NUMERIC(15,0)` since Vietnamese Dong doesn't have fractional units. This inconsistency could cause formatting issues if the application rounds amounts differently than the database expects. I should update the spec to align with the current database implementation rather than forcing a schema change.\n\nA more critical issue is that the `reviews` table is missing a `tutor_id` foreign key entirely, which breaks the ability to associate reviews with specific tutors. This affects the Tutor Center's rating display and the matching algorithm.\n\nThe current workaround hardcodes zero ratings because there's no way to query reviews by tutor. The proper fix requires adding `tutor_id UUID REFERENCES users(id)` to the reviews table and reconsidering the `reviewer_id ON DELETE SET NULL` behavior — setting a reviewer to null destroys the audit trail, so we should either anonymize the reviewer or implement soft deletes instead. This architectural gap needs to be resolved as part of the Epic 0 P0 fixes before the Tutor Center can display accurate ratings.",
